# 📊 PATTERN ANALYSIS & STATISTICS

## Test Structure (Consistent 2012–2024)
- **Total MCQs:** 100 (split into 2 sections)
- **Section 1 — Science Achievement:** ~50 MCQs (Biology ~20, Chemistry ~20, Physics ~10-15)
- **Section 2 — Science & Math Reasoning:** ~50 MCQs (Science Reasoning ~25, Math Reasoning ~25)
- **Duration:** 2 hours (120 minutes)
- **Negative Marking:** -0.25 for Bio, Chem, Physics ONLY | No negative marking in Reasoning

## Most Repeated Topics (Based on 2012–2024 Analysis)

### BIOLOGY — Top Repeated Topics
1. **Blood Groups & Genetics** — appeared EVERY year (ABO system, codominance, pedigree) 🔥🔥🔥
2. **Cell Biology** — organelles, membrane transport, mitochondria 🔥🔥
3. **Excretory System** — nephron structure, reabsorption, urea 🔥🔥
4. **Photosynthesis** — light reactions, C3 vs C4, chlorophyll types 🔥🔥
5. **Reproduction** — menstrual cycle, meiosis vs mitosis, gametogenesis 🔥
6. **Enzymes** — mechanism, inhibition (appears in Reasoning section too) 🔥
7. **DNA/Molecular Bio** — replication, transcription, PCR 🔥

### CHEMISTRY — Top Repeated Topics
1. **Organic Chemistry** — benzene reactions, nitration, Friedel-Crafts 🔥🔥🔥
2. **Bonding & VSEPR** — hybridization, sigma/pi bonds, molecular geometry 🔥🔥
3. **Chemical Equilibrium** — Kc, Kp, Le Chatelier's 🔥🔥
4. **Electrochemistry** — redox, oxidation states, electrochemical cells 🔥🔥
5. **Stoichiometry** — mole concept, empirical formula, limiting reagent 🔥
6. **Acids/Bases/Buffers** — Henderson equation, pH calculations 🔥

### PHYSICS — Top Repeated Topics
1. **Current Electricity** — Ohm's law, circuits, resistor combinations 🔥🔥🔥
2. **Thermodynamics** — 1st/2nd law, PV diagrams, processes 🔥🔥
3. **SHM & Waves** — pendulum, frequency, Young's double slit 🔥🔥
4. **Electromagnetism** — force on conductor, magnetic field, Faraday's law 🔥🔥
5. **Modern/Nuclear Physics** — alpha/beta decay, half-life, Einstein 🔥
6. **Optics** — critical angle, diffraction, interference 🔥

### MATH REASONING — Top Repeated Topics
1. **Percentages & Ratios** — direct calculation, word problems 🔥🔥🔥
2. **Algebra** — simplification, equations, sequences 🔥🔥
3. **Geometry** — area, perimeter, cubes, angles 🔥🔥
4. **Quantitative Comparison** — Quantity A vs B problems (GRE-style) 🔥
5. **Logic & Sets** — Venn diagrams, calendar problems 🔥

## BIO-01 — Cell Biology & Organelles

---

🔥 **Q1.** ⭐ Some cellular organelles are bound by a single membrane, while other organelles have two membranes around them. Which of the following is correct?
| | Single Membrane | Double Membrane |
|---|---|---|
| A | Peroxisome, Lysosome | Nucleus, Chloroplast |
| B | Chloroplast, Lysosome | Nucleus, Peroxisome |
| C | Peroxisome, Chloroplast | Lysosome, Nucleus |
**Answer: A** [Official Sample]

---

🔥 **Q2.** [ 2021] Which of the following options is CORRECT regarding organelle classification?
| | Non-membrane bound | Single-membrane bound | Double-membrane bound |
|---|---|---|---|
| A | Nucleolus | Centrosome | Nucleus |
| B | Mitochondria | Vacuole | Nucleus |
| C | Ribosome | Lysosome | Chloroplast |
| D | Microtubules | Peroxisome | Lysosome |
**Answer: C** [REPEATED — similar concept in official sample]

---

**Q3.** ⭐ Which of the following processes is used to isolate various components of the cell?
- A. Magnification
- B. Fractionation
- C. Resolution
**Answer: B** [Official Sample]

---

**Q4.** [ 2012] Where in the mitochondrion do the reactions of the electron transfer system (chain) occur?
- A. On the inner membrane
- B. On the outer membrane
- C. In the mitochondrial ribosomes
- D. Between the outer and inner membrane
**Answer: A**

---

🔥 **Q5.** [ 2012] [REPEATED 2x: 2012, 2024] Which of the following human cells do NOT contain mitochondria?
- A. Nerve Cell
- B. Red Blood Cell
- C. Liver Cell
- D. White Blood Cell
**Answer: B**

---

**Q6.** [ 2020] Which of the following is the function of nucleohistones?
- A. DNA replication
- B. Condensing information
**Answer: B**

---

**Q7.** [ 2024] A girl is examining the transport of material across the cytoplasm and nucleus. Which is responsible for the transport of small polar molecules?
- A. Membrane channels
- B. Nuclear complexes like kinesins
- C. Nuclear pores
- D. Intermembrane transport
**Answer: C** [REPEATED 2x: 2024 recreated versions]

---

**Q8.** [ 2022] Correct pathway of transport of protein from ER to outside the cell:
- A. Ribosome → lumen of RER → Golgi complex → plasma membrane → outside the cell
**Answer: A**

---

**Q9.** [ 2023] Picture about electron transport chain was given — what is the final usage of H⁺ and electrons?
- A. Combine with oxygen to form water
- B. Recycled through cytochrome complexes
- C. Used to reduce NADH
**Answer: A**

## BIO-02 — Cell Cycle, Mitosis & Meiosis

---

⭐ **Q10.** In the diagram showing the cell cycle, G1 and G2 represents periods of intensive synthesis. Which correctly identifies the cellular events associated with mitosis?
- A. Cytokinesis → Prophase → Telophase
- B. Cytokinesis → Metaphase → Telophase
- C. DNA Synthesis → Metaphase → Cytokinesis
**Answer: C** [Official Sample] [DIAGRAM]

---

⭐ **Q11.** The following graph represents the changes in quantity of DNA at different stages in a cell cycle. Which stage is represented by "X"?
- A. Replication
- B. Prophase
- C. Cytokinesis
**Answer: C** [Official Sample] [DIAGRAM]

---

**Q12.** ⭐ What does mitosis produce to assist in the maintenance of health?
- A. Gametes to ensure reproduction
- B. Polypeptides to ensure cell growth
- C. Prions to protect cells against cancer
**Answer: B** [Official Sample]

---

🔥 **Q13.** [ 2024] Mitosis is a process in which cells:
- A. Form specialized structures
- B. Increase in chromosome number
- C. Grow
- D. Multiply
**Answer: D** [REPEATED 2x: Both 2024 versions]

---

**Q14.** [ 2022] What is the main difference between prophase of meiosis and mitosis?
- A. In meiosis crossing over occurs whereas in mitosis this does not occur
- B. In meiosis centromere does not separate
**Answer: A**

---

🔥 **Q15.** ⭐ In a plant where 2n = 24, what will be the total number of chromatids present during prophase 1 of meiosis?
- A. 12
- B. 24
- C. 48
**Answer: C** [Official Sample]

---

**Q16.** [ 2022] A cell has Y amount of DNA when it is just divided. The relative amount of DNA in prophase of meiosis will be:
- A. Y
- B. 2Y
- C. Y/2
- D. Y/4
**Answer: B**

## BIO-03 — Genetics & Inheritance

---

🔥🔥🔥 **NOTE:** This is THE most tested topic. Blood groups, codominance, pedigrees, and Mendelian crosses appear EVERY SINGLE YEAR.

---

🔥 **Q17.** [ 2012] [REPEATED 5x: 2012, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2024] If brown eyes (B) is dominant to blue eyes (b), what is the genotype of the parents? [DIAGRAM — pedigree given]
- A. Father Bb, mother BB
- B. Father BB, mother Bb
- C. Father Bb, mother Bb
- D. Father bb, mother bb
**Answer: C**

---

🔥 **Q18.** ⭐ [REPEATED 4x] The diagram shows a cross resulting in incomplete dominance. What will be the genotypic ratio?
- A. 1:2:1
- B. 3:1
- C. 1:1
**Answer: A** [Official Sample]

---

🔥 **Q19.** ⭐ The pedigree chart shows a possible pattern of inheritance for human albinism. Which of the following is correct?
- A. People with albinism are homozygous for this disease.
- B. The gene for normal skin pigmentation is recessive.
- C. There are two genes that code for albinism.
**Answer: A** [Official Sample]

---

🔥 **Q20.** ⭐ The offspring in F1 generation have 50% chance for each of the blood groups A and AB. What is the genotype of their parents?
**Answer:** [Requires diagram — IAIA × IAi or similar cross] [Official Sample]

---

🔥 **Q21.** [ 2012] The universal recipients (Group AB) can receive blood from any other blood group because:
- A. They do not suffer from anemia.
- B. They receive more blood than other people.
- C. Their blood contains neither A nor B antibodies.
- D. Their blood contains neither A nor B antigens.
**Answer: C**

---

🔥 **Q22.** [ 2021] If a mother has the blood group AB and the father has the blood type O, what could the blood group of the children be?
- A. A and B
- B. A, B, and O
- C. A only
- D. A and O only
**Answer: A**

---

🔥 **Q23.** [ 2024] [REPEATED 2x] Which of the following characteristics applies to individuals with blood type O?
- A. A and B antigens
- B. A and B antibodies in plasma
- C. Anti-A in RBC
- D. Antigen B only
**Answer: B**

---

🔥 **Q24.** [ 2022] A fetus's mother's blood group is O-ve (dd) while father is B+ (homozygous for Rh DD). What will be the chances of erythroblastosis fetalis?
- A. 0%
- B. 25%
- C. 50%
- D. 100%
**Answer: D** (Father DD, all offspring Dd = Rh+)

---

**Q25.** [ 2022] Purple × white flower (Pp × pp). Chances of white flower:
**Answer: 50%**

---

**Q26.** [ 2021] The valley rose (R) is completely dominant to mountain rose (r) and pink color (P) is completely dominant to white (p). RRPp × rrpp. Phenotypic ratio?

- C. 1:1:1:1
- D. 1 valley white : 1 mountain pink
**Answer: B**

---

🔥 **Q27.** [ 2023] Which blood group does NOT exhibit codominance?
- A. A Blood Group
- B. B Blood Group
- C. M Blood Group
- D. O Blood Group
**Answer: D**

⭐ **Q28.** A person with Turner's syndrome lacks:

- B. One X chromosome

**Q30.** [ 2021] What type of disease? [Pedigree diagram given]

## BIO-04 — DNA, Molecular Biology & Biotechnology

---

⭐ **Q31.** The diagram shows structure of DNA with complementary pairing. Which chemical bond is formed between two nucleotides of a strand?
- A. Hydrogen bond
- B. Sigma bond
- C. Double bond
**Answer: B (Phosphodiester bond — sigma bond)** [Official Sample]

---

⭐ **Q32.** The diagram shows steps of DNA replication. Which is the correct order?
- A. i → ii → iii
- B. i → iii → ii
- C. ii → iii → i
**Answer:** [DIAGRAM-BASED] [Official Sample]

---

🔥 **Q33.** [ 2012] A mRNA is 999 nucleotides long. The number of amino acids in the polypeptide chain will be:
- A. 111
- B. 333
- C. 666
- D. 1998
**Answer: B** (999/3 = 333)

---

🔥 **Q34.** [ 2022] A polypeptide consists of 250 amino acids. How many nucleotides in the mRNA?
- A. 200
- B. 250
- C. 450
- D. 750
**Answer: D** (250 × 3 = 750)

---

**Q35.** [ 2012] Bacteria were cultured in medium containing heavy nitrogen (¹⁵N). The percentage of cells containing ¹⁵N in each generation was estimated. Which curve provides evidence that DNA replication is semi-conservative?
**Answer:** [DIAGRAM-BASED]

---

**Q36.** [ 2012] A fragment of DNA, cut by a restriction enzyme, forms bonds with other DNA molecules that have:
- A. Been fragmented by the same restriction enzyme
- B. Sticky ends
- C. Plasmid components
- D. Been attached to methyl group
**Answer: A**

---

⭐ **Q37.** What is the role of "primers" in PCR?
- A. They are required by an enzyme to start the process.
- B. They provide suitable temperature for the reaction.
- C. They are enzymes that carry out DNA replication.
**Answer: A** [Official Sample]

---

⭐ **Q38.** Why is the use of bacteria preferred in genetic engineering for insulin production?
- A. Each bacterial cell is capable of producing insulin itself.
- B. Bacterial cell provides catalyst for insulin production.
- C. A simpler version of human insulin molecule is produced.
**Answer: C** [Official Sample]

---

⭐ **Q39.** A plant cell whose cell wall is removed for producing transgenic plants is known as:
- A. Nucleoplast
- B. Protoplast
- C. Cytoplasm
**Answer: B** [Official Sample]

---

⭐ **Q40.** The diagram shows ex-vivo gene therapy. A person with SCID lacks which enzyme?
- A. Reverse transcriptase
- B. Adenylate cyclase
- C. Adenosine deaminase
**Answer: C** [Official Sample]

---

**Q41.** [ 2021] Why is rRNA used as a molecular clock instead of tRNA?
- A. tRNA contains deoxyribose sugar groups
- B. rRNA is double stranded
- C. tRNA is difficult to measure in the lab
- D. rRNA is more stable
**Answer: D**

- D. Right atrium → right ventricle → lungs → left atrium → left ventricle

- B. Tissue fluid

**Answer: A**

**Q59.** [ 2024] Which is NOT a similarity between hemoglobin and myoglobin?

- B. sp²

🔥 **Q195.** [ 2024] [REPEATED 2x] What can penetrate uranium nucleus?
- A. Electron
- B. Lepton
- C. Alpha Particle
- D. Neutron
**Answer: D**

# ━━━━━━━━━━━━ MATHEMATICS REASONING ━━━━━━━━━━━━

## MATH-01 — Percentages & Basic Arithmetic

---

⭐ **Q196.** Which has the least value?
- A. 1/4
- B. 3/8
- C. 2/11
- D. 1/5
**Answer: C** [Official Sample]

---

⭐ **Q197.** If 30% of 30 is 9, then 110% of 110 is:
- A. 64
- B. 81
- C. 121
- D. 110
**Answer: C** [Official Sample]

---

🔥 **Q198.** [ 2021] How many times should 0.5 be added to itself to get 50?
- A. 50
- B. 51
- C. 99
- D. 100
**Answer: C** (0.5 + 0.5×99 = 50)

---

**Q199.** [ 2024] If a person bought a dozen oranges for 1200 and makes 36 Rs profit on each, percentage profit:
**Answer: 36%**

---

🔥 **Q200.** [ 2024] [REPEATED 2x] In office, 78% use laptops, 43% use iPads. % using both:
- A. 21%
- B. 40%
- C. 30%
**Answer: A** (78 + 43 - 100 = 21%)

---

## MATH-02 — Algebra & Equations

---

⭐ **Q201.** What is the 10th term: 3, 7, 11, 15, 19, 23...
- A. 27
- B. 35
- C. 39
- D. 43
**Answer: C** [Official Sample] (a + (n-1)d = 3 + 9×4 = 39)

---

🔥 **Q202.** [ 2012] [REPEATED 2x: 2012, 2021] Simplify 8×n − 18 + 6 − 5×n:
- A. 3(n − 12)
- B. 3(n + 4)
- C. 3(n − 4)
- D. 3(n − 8)
**Answer: C** (3n − 12 = 3(n − 4))

---

**Q203.** [ 2012] Solid cube 6×6×6 cut into 216 small cubes (1×1×1), painted on all faces. How many small cubes painted on at least 2 sides?
- A. 56
- B. 45
- C. 28
- D. 23
**Answer: A**

---

## MATH-03 — Geometry & Mensuration

---

⭐ **Q204.** Length of rope for boundaries of square with area 64 m²:
- A. 32m
- B. 32m²
- C. 64m
- D. 16m
**Answer: A** [Official Sample] (side=8, perimeter=32)

---

⭐ **Q205.** If cube A has side 2cm and cube B side 6cm, how many A cubes fit in B?
- A. 9
- B. 15
- C. 18
- D. 27
**Answer: D** [Official Sample] (6³/2³ = 216/8 = 27)

---

**Q206.** [ 2023] Perimeter of octagon with sides of 7cm:
**Answer: 56 cm** (8 × 7 = 56)

---

## MATH-04 — Ratios, Proportions & Word Problems

---

⭐ **Q207.** There are 108 passengers. Ratio of males to females is 5:4. Number of females:
- A. 46
- B. 48
- C. 60
- D. 54
**Answer: B** [Official Sample] (108 × 4/9 = 48)

---

🔥 **Q208.** [ 2012] [REPEATED 2x: 2012, 2021] A, B, C, D, E play cards. A says to B: "If you give me 3, you'll have as many as E; if I give you 3, you'll have as many as D." A and B together have 10 more than D and E. B has 2 more than C. Total = 133. How many does B have?
- A. 22
- B. 23
- C. 25
- D. 35
**Answer: C**

---

**Q209.** [ 2024] If ratio of girls to boys is 5:2 and 30 girls present, how many boys?
**Answer: 12**

---

## MATH-05 — Quantitative Comparison (GRE-style)

---

**Q210.** [ 2012] If x is integer and 1 < x < 3:
- Quantity A = x²
- Quantity B = 2x
- A. A > B
- B. B > A
- C. Equal
- D. Cannot be determined
**Answer: C** (x=2, so A=4, B=4)

---

**Q211.** [ 2012] If x and y are prime and x+y=12:
- Quantity A = x
- Quantity B = 8
**Answer: D** (Cannot be determined — could be 5+7 or 7+5)

---

**Q212.** [ 2012] Perimeter of square = circumference of circle:
- Quantity A = area of circle
- Quantity B = area of square
**Answer: A** (Circle always has larger area for same perimeter)

## 🎯 KEY TAKEAWAY FOR EXAM PREP

> **If you master these ~500 questions, you will have seen virtually every type of MCQ that has ever asked.** Focus especially on the 🔥 marked questions — these are the patterns keeps repeating.

> **Golden Rule:** loves CONCEPTUAL questions. They rarely ask for pure recall. Instead, they test WHETHER YOU UNDERSTAND the concept by applying it in a slightly different way each year.

## EXTRA BIOLOGY — From Undated Past Papers

**Q243.** Potato arises from:
- A. Primary root
- B. Lateral root
- C. Stem branches
- D. Adventitious root
**Answer: C** (Potato is a stem tuber)

**Q244.** When given a math question, which part of the brain works?
- A. Diencephalon
- B. Medulla Oblongata
- C. Telencephalon (cerebrum)
- D. Cerebellum
**Answer: C**

---

**Q245.** Hemophilia — parents have hemophilic son, normal son, and hemophilic daughter. Genotype of parents?
- A. XᴴXᴴ × XʰY
- B. XᴴXʰ × XᴴY
- C. XᴴXʰ × XʰY
- D. XʰXʰ × XᴴY
**Answer: C** (For hemophilic daughter, mother must be carrier XᴴXʰ AND father must be Xʰ Y)

---

**Q246.** Seedless fruits are formed by application of:

- B. Ethene
- C. Vernilline
- D. Auxin
**Answer: D** (Parthenocarpy induced by auxin)

- (ii) Organisms vary
- (iii) Some variants better adapted
- (iv) Struggle for existence

- (vi) Heritable variations will predominate
- A. iv,v,i,iii,ii,vi
- B. v,iv,ii,iii,i,vi
- C. iv,v,i,ii,iii,vi
- D. v,vi,ii,iii,i,iv
**Answer: B**

**Q355.** [ Past Paper]
An inelastic wire is used to pull a bead that is threaded in a rod. The force applied by the wire
is 10N and the angle between the wire and the rod is 60 degrees. What is the work done in
pulling the bead over a distance of 8m.
- A. 40J
(Work = Force * Distance * cosθ
where θ is the angle between the force and the direction of movement.
Substituting the values, we get:
Work = 10 N * 8 m * cos 60°
= 10 N * 8 m * 0.5
= 40 J
Therefore, the work done in pulling the bead over a distance of 8 m is 40 J.)

**Q379.** [ Past Paper]
What is the pathway of impulse in a simple reflex arc?

- B. Sensory neuron, receptor, interneuron, effector, motor neuron
- C. Sensory neuron, receptor, interneuron, motor neuron, effector
Answer: A) Receptor, sensory neuron, interneuron, motor neuron, effector
Explanation: This is the correct pathway for a simple reflex arc. In a simple reflex arc, the
sensory neuron transmits the signal from the receptor to the interneuron in the spinal cord,
which then relays it to the motor neuron, causing the effector muscle to contract.

**Q381.** [ Past Paper]
The dry weight of a seedling is reduced during germination due to?
Options:
- A. Respiration
- B. Transpiration
- C. Photosynthesis
- D. Formation of new tissues
Correct Answer:
- A. Respiration
Explanation:
During germination, the stored nutrients in the seed are metabolized to provide energy for
growth. This metabolic process, known as respiration, involves the breakdown of stored food
substances into simpler molecules, releasing energy, carbon dioxide, and water. This
breakdown and utilization of stored food cause a decrease in the seedling's dry weight, as some
of the material is converted to gases and lost to the atmosphere. Hence, respiration is the
primary reason for the reduction in dry weight during germination.
---

---

- C. Remains the same
Answer: A) Decreases by 500 J
Explanation: The change in internal energy includes work done and heat
absorbed. The calculation depends on the values of pressure and volume
change.
---

---

**Q388.** [ Past Paper]
Young’s Double Slit Experiment:
Question: Which of the following will increase the separation between dark
fringes in Young's double-slit experiment?
Options:
- A. Use red light
- B. Use blue light
- C. Increase distance between screen and slits
- D. Decrease distance between screen and slits
Answer: A) Use red light and C) Increase distance between screen and slits
Explanation: Using red light (longer wavelength) increases the fringe spacing,
and increasing the distance between the screen and slits increases the path
difference, thus increasing fringe separation.
---

---

**Q389.** [ Past Paper]
The radius of the circular path of a charged particle in a magnetic field is?
Options:
- A. Directly proportional to the charge of the particle
- B. Inversely proportional to the mass of the particle
- C. Directly proportional to the velocity of the particle
- D. Directly proportional to the mass of the particle
Correct Answer:
- D. Directly proportional to the mass of the particle
Explanation:
When a charged particle moves through a magnetic field, it experiences a force perpendicular to
both its velocity and the magnetic field, causing it to move in a circular path. The radius rrr of
this path is given by the formula:
where m is the mass of the particle, v is its velocity, q is the charge, and B is the magnetic field
strength. From this equation, it is evident that the radius of the path is directly proportional to the
mass of the particle mmm. Thus, as the mass increases, the radius also increases, provided
other factors remain constant.
---

**Q390.** [ Past Paper]
Critical Angle and Light Path:
○
Question: A light ray incident at 40° with a critical angle of 42°. What happens?
○
Options:
- A. Total internal reflection along Q
- B. Passing straight through boundary along P
- C. Along path Q with no internal reflection
○
Answer: B) Passing straight through boundary along P
○
Explanation: Since the incident angle is less than the critical angle, the light
passes through the medium without total internal reflection.
○

○

**Q393.** [ Past Paper]
Hydrolysis of Alkyne:
●
Question: What intermediate unstable compound is formed during the hydrolysis of
alkyne in the presence of H₂SO₄?
●
Options:
- A. Oxalic acid
- B. Vinyl alcohol
- C. Formaldehyde
●
Answer: B) Vinyl alcohol
●
Explanation: Vinyl alcohol is an unstable intermediate formed during the hydration of
alkynes, which quickly tautomerizes to form aldehydes or ketones.

**Q394.** [ Past Paper]
Boiling Point of Alcohols:
●
Question: Which of the following alcohols has the lowest boiling point?
●
Options:
- A. Primary butanol
- B. Secondary butanol
- C. Tertiary butanol
●
Answer: C) Tertiary butanol
●
Explanation: Tertiary butanol has the lowest boiling point due to less effective hydrogen
bonding compared to primary and secondary alcohols.

**Q395.** [ Past Paper]
Young's Double Slit Experiment:
●
Question: Which of the following will increase the separation between dark fringes?
●
Options:
- A. Use red light
- B. Use blue light
- C. Increase distance between screen and slits
- D. Decrease distance between screen and slits
●
Answer: A) Use red light and C) Increase distance between screen and slits

between the screen and slits increases the path difference, thus increasing the fringe
spacing.

**Q396.** [ Past Paper]
Extraction of Bauxite Ore:
●
Question: By which process is bauxite ore extracted?
●
Options:
- A. Electrolysis
- B. Smelting
- C. Reduction by carbon
●
Answer: A) Electrolysis
●
Explanation: Bauxite is the primary ore of aluminum, and it is refined through
electrolysis in the Hall-Héroult process to produce aluminum metal.

**Q397.** [ Past Paper]
Force Required to Slide Layers of Liquid:
●
Question: What is the force required to slide one layer of a liquid over another?
●
Options:
- A. Viscosity
- B. Upthrust
- C. Drag
●
Answer: A) Viscosity
●
Explanation: Viscosity is the measure of a fluid's resistance to gradual deformation by
shear or tensile stress. It quantifies the internal frictional force between the layers of a
fluid in motion.

Questions 66 and 67 refer to the following passage.
Ionizing radiation refers to energy emitted in the form of particles or electromagnetic waves that
is strong enough to remove electrons from atoms or molecules. This process, called ionization,
can affect biological tissues when organisms are exposed to such radiation. Sources of ionizing
radiation include certain radioactive materials, cosmic rays, and medical imaging technologies.
The biological impact of this radiation depends on factors such as dose, exposure time, and the
type of tissue affected. Scientific studies have explored how cells respond to ionizing radiation
and the various ways it may influence cellular structures and functions.
---

---

Questions 68 to 70 refer to the following passage.
In the study of heredity, scientists examine how traits are passed from parents to offspring. This
area of biology considers patterns of inheritance and how genetic information is transmitted
across generations. Early research in this field involved the use of model organisms to track the
appearance of observable traits. By analyzing these patterns, researchers began to develop
principles that explain how traits can appear in some generations but not others. Today, these
principles continue to inform modern genetics and our understanding of inheritance in both
plants and animals.
---

---

A pressure-volume (P-V) graph is a useful tool in thermodynamics for visualizing how the
pressure of a gas changes with its volume during various processes. Each point on the graph
represents a specific state of the gas, defined by its pressure and volume. Different curves on
the graph can correspond to different types of thermodynamic processes, such as isothermal,
isobaric, or adiabatic changes. The area under a curve on a P-V graph often represents the
work done by or on the gas during a process. By analyzing these graphs, one can gain insights
into the behavior of gases and the energy transformations taking place. The following diagram
shows a P-V graph.

accounted for by the ideal gas equation (General gas equation). The molecules of an ideal gas do not attract or
repel one another, and their volume is negligible compared with the volume of the container. Experiments
show that under the STP conditions, one mole of an ideal gas occupies a volume of 22.4dm3.

Question 33-35 refer to the following information:
Increase in height
It is generally well known that on average we are taller than our ancestor; this is true of people in various
places around the world.
The people of the Netherlands, now the world’s tallest, have been on an upward trend since 1851, though
the rate of growth accelerated after the second world war. In 1965, the average Dutchman was 1.78m
tall. By 1997, he was 6cm taller. The average Dutch woman grew from 1.66m to 1.71m.
The Spanish grew 2.4cm per decade between 1960 and 1990. In Western Europe this growth has now
slowed to about 1cm per decade but Western Europe and Japan are increasing by up to 3cm per decade.
However, on average, women are increasing in height more slowly than men so the gap in height between
the sexes is widening.
It has been found that it is the first two years of life which are the most important for height, when the leg
bones are growing rapidly. Not only increased food supply but also general health improvement brought
about antibiotics and vaccinations have had an effect. Your final height, however, is still dependent on the
height of your parents and grandparents.
---

---

**Q1216.** [ Past Paper]
A farmer sold apples, pears, and tomatoes by the kilogram for a total receipt of Rs. 480.00. How
many kilograms of apples did the farmer sell?
question?
I.
Apples and pears were each sold at Rs.0.50 per kilogram
II.
A total of 780 kilograms of pears and tomatoes was sold.
III.
The total receipt for apples was equal to the combined receipt for pears and tomatoes.
IV.
The total receipt for apples was 4 times the total receipt for pears.
V.
The total receipt for tomatoes was 3 times the total receipt for pears.
- A. I and IV
- B. II and IV
- C. III and V
- D. I and III
Answer: D
